Produces dialogue and a persistent session file — not code or files. Works across domains: product design, software architecture, business strategy, creative writing, research proposals. Do NOT use for code review, debugging, refactoring, implementation tasks, or general feedback on existing code. - **grill-me**: Stress-test a plan or design through relentless questioning until every branch of the decision tree is resolved.
